# Skills · A · part 7

810 entries, adev:assess to adr-assistant. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **adev:assess**: Assess codebase readiness for agentic development. Scans the codebase using static file inspection and outputs maturity scores across 8 structural dimensions (raw mode) or 11 dimensions (adev mode). Use when you want to evaluate how well-suited a codebase is for AI-assisted development, identify improvement areas, or track progress over time. ([agentic-development/adev-plugin](https://github.com/agentic-development/adev-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/assess) · ★13)
